      細分賽道競爭加劇，數智化轉型全面提速，資產           brands accelerating; service and experience-oriented
      組合集約化和運營服務精益化成為大勢所趨。面           consumption remains active, while high-end consumption
      對行業新格局，本集團秉持「外延式拓展 +內涵          has gradually stabilized. The “Property Service
      式增長」雙輪驅動策略，堅持固本強基、深化創           Improvement Initiative” has been included in the national
      新轉型，在複雜多變的市場環境中展現了強勁韌           15th Five-Year Plan. Competition in segmented market
      性。期內，實現綜合收入人民幣92.17 億元，同        tracks has intensified, digital-intelligent transformation is

五、 未來展望                   V.    FUTURE OUTLOOK
當前，中國經濟錨定「高質量發展」主線，持續     At present, the Chinese economy is anchored on the main
釋放內需潛力和產業升級紅利。商管行業正從      theme of “high-quality development”, continuing to unleash
規模擴張轉向存量深耕，品牌資源的掌控力與空     the potential of domestic demand and the dividends
間內容的創造力成為構建競爭壁壘的核心因素；     of industrial upgrading. The commercial management
物管行業全面邁入質效升級階段，精細化運營與     industry is shifting from scale expansion to deep cultivation
系統性成本管控能力成為決定盈利邊界的關鍵變     of existing stock, where the control of brand resources
量。同時，以大模型為代表的AI技術正在加速重    and the creativity of spatial content have become the core
構空間管理、流量經營與數據價值挖掘的底層邏     factors for building competitive barriers. The property
輯，會員資產從輔助工具升級為核心競爭力。      management industry has comprehensively entered the
                          stage of quality and efficiency upgrades, where refined
                          operations and systematic cost control capabilities have
                          become key variables determining profitability boundaries.
                          Meanwhile, AI technology, represented by large models,
                          is accelerating the restructuring of the underlying logic
                          of space management, traffic operation, and data value
                          excavation, upgrading membership assets from auxiliary
                          tools to core competitiveness.

未來展望                      FUTURE OUTLOOK
隨着房地產行業進入存量提質增效的新階段，本     As the real estate industry enters a new stage
集團作為輕資產運營型企業，迎來全面高質量發     characterized by the enhancement of quality and efficiency
展的重要戰略機遇期。「十五五」期間，本集團將    in respect of existing stock, the Group, as a light-asset
繼續堅定商管、物管和大會員一體化「2+1」業務   operating enterprise, ushers in an important period of

      核心淨利潤（非香港財務報告會計準                CORE NET PROFIT (NON-HKFRS
      則計量）                            ACCOUNTING STANDARDS MEASURE)
      為補充按照香港財務報告會計準則編製的綜合財           To supplement the consolidated financial statements,
      務報表，本集團亦採用了非香港財務報告會計準           which are presented in accordance with HKFRS
      則規定的或按其呈列的核心淨利潤（非香港財務           Accounting Standards, the Group also uses core net
      報告會計準則計量）作為附加財務計量。本集團           profit (non-HKFRS Accounting Standards measure) as
      認為，通過撇除與公司日常業務運營及管理無關           an additional financial measure, which is not required
      的若干項目的潛在影響，該非香港財務報告會計           by, or presented in accordance with HKFRS Accounting
      準則計量的呈現可為投資者及管理層提供了解集           Standards. The Group believes that the presentation of
      團各期核心業務綜合業績的有用資料。核心淨利           such non-HKFRS Accounting Standards measure provides
      潤（非香港財務報告會計準則計量）界定為扣除           useful information to investors and the management
      投資物業的重估收益 ╱ 虧損、無形資產 — 客戶        in understanding the consolidated results of the
      關係攤銷及處置、或然代價的公允價值變動收            core operation of the Group from period to period by
      益 ╱ 虧損及相關遞延稅項影響而調整後的本集          excluding the potential impact of certain items that are
      團股東應佔淨利潤。                       unrelated to the Company’s daily business operations
                                      and management. The core net profit (non-HKFRS
                                      Accounting Standards measure) is defined as the net
                                      profit attributable to shareholders of the Group adjusted
                                      by deducting revaluation gain/loss from investment
                                      properties, amortisation and disposal of intangible assets
                                      — customer relationships, gain/loss on changes in fair
                                      value of contingent consideration and associated deferred
                                      tax impact.

有效淨現金流（非香港財務報告會計            EFFECTIVE NET CASH FLOW (NON-
準則計量）                       HKFRS ACCOUNTING STANDARDS
                            MEASURE)
為補充根據香港財務報告會計準則編製之綜合財       To supplement the consolidated financial statements,
務報表，本集團亦採用「有效淨現金流」作為附       which are presented in accordance with HKFRS
加財務計量指標。該指標並非香港財務報告會計       Accounting Standards, the Group also use “effective
準則所界定或呈列，旨在通過對比核心淨利潤與       net cash flow” as an additional financial measure. This
現金流，為投資者及管理層提供有關本集團業績       measure is not defined or presented under HKFRS
質量的有用參考。                    Accounting Standards, which is intended to provide
                            investors and the management with a useful reference on
                            the quality of the Group’s results by comparing core net
                            profit with cash flow.


本集團將有效淨現金流界定為：經營活動所得現       The Group defines the effective net cash flow as: net cash
金淨額，經調整還原併購項目化債對抵的經營欠       generated from operating activities, adjusted to reinstate
款影響，並計入核心淨利潤口徑下的利息流入後       the effect of operating receivables offset against debt
的現金流狀況。其中，併購化債對抵的經營欠款       assumed in acquisitions, and incorporating the cash flow
指本集團在併購化債中，以應付股轉款對抵應收       position after interest inflows under the core net profit
經營欠款。該抵銷處理導致本集團投資活動現金       measurement. Among them, operating receivables offset
流流出及經營活動現金流流入同時減少。管理層       against debt assumed in acquisitions refer to operating
認為，將該部分款項還原至經營活動，能更準確       receivables offset by the Group against share transfer
地反映本集團真實的現金創造能力。            payables in debt assumption transactions. Such offsetting
                            treatment results in a simultaneous reduction in both
                            cash outflows from investing activities and cash inflows
                            from operating activities of the Group. The management
                            is of the view that reinstating such amounts to operating
                            activities can more accurately reflect the Group’s actual
                            cash-generating ability.

人民幣1,582.7 百萬元。該指標由經營活動所得   measure) was RMB1,582.7 million. This indicator was
現金淨額人民幣1,522.3 百萬元，加上核心淨利   calculated based on net cash generated from operating
潤口徑下的利息流入人民幣 60.4 百萬元計算得    activities of RMB1,522.3 million, adding interest inflows
出。以此計算，有效淨現金流對核心淨利潤的覆       under the core net profit measurement of RMB60.4
蓋倍數為0.71倍，同比提升0.02倍，在市場下行   million. Based on the above calculation, the coverage

      外匯風險                            FOREIGN CURRENCY RISK
      由於本集團業務主要於中國進行，本集團主要採           As the Group’s business is mainly conducted in the PRC,
      用人民幣作為結算貨幣。截至二零二六年六月            the Group mainly adopts RMB as the settlement currency.
      三十日止，非人民幣資產及負債主要為現金港幣           As of 30 June 2026, non-RMB assets and liabilities mainly
      1.1 百萬元、19,518.0 美元。管理層認為，本集    included cash of HKD1.1 million and US$19,518.0. The
      團主要業務活動均以人民幣結算，因此人民幣匯           management considers that fluctuation of RMB exchange
      率波動不會對本集團的財務狀況及經營業績產生           rate will not have a material impact on the Group’s
      重大影響。基於當前的業務結構，本集團暫未制           financial position and results of operations, as the Group’s
      定專門的外幣風險對沖政策，然而，管理層將對           principal business activities are settled in RMB. In light
      外匯風險敞口實施動態監控並將根據市場環境的           of the current business structure, the Group has not yet
      變化進行必要調整。                       formulated any specific hedging policies against its foreign
                                      exchange risk, but the management will actively monitor
                                      the foreign exchange exposure and make necessary
                                      adjustments in accordance with the changes in market
                                      environment.

      更換獨立核數師                         CHANGE OF INDEPENDENT AUDITOR
      畢馬威會計師事務所自二零二三年六月起擔任本           KPMG had served as the independent auditor of the
      公司獨立核數師，並已於二零二六年六月八日召           Company since June 2023, and retired from office upon
      開的本公司股東週年大會結束後屆滿退任。本公           the conclusion of the annual general meeting of the
      司及華潤（集團）均未訂立規定獨立核數師固定           Company held on 8 June 2026. The Company and CRH
      任期或強制輪替週期的獨立書面政策。此外，退           do not have any standalone written policy prescribing a
      任獨立核數師的服務任期亦尚未達到中華人民共           fixed tenure or mandatory cycle for change of independent
      和國國務院國有資產監督管理委員會相關法規規           auditor and the service tenure of the outgoing independent
      定的上限。儘管如此，考慮到現行市場狀況及近           auditor has not reached the limit prescribed in the
      期全球經濟逆風，為符合良好企業管治標準、提           relevant regulations issued by the State-owned Assets
      升核數師的獨立性與客觀性，並防範因長期合作           Supervision and Administration Commission of the State
      關係隨時間推移可能產生的熟悉度威脅及因商業           Council of the People’s Republic of China. However,
      動態而產生的相關履職風險，華潤（集團）採取           taking into account the prevailing market conditions and
      了更為審慎的做法。作為良好企業管治實務的舉           headwinds of recent global economy and to promote good
      措，華潤（集團）建議在完成三個審計週期後更           corporate governance and maintain the independence and
      換本公司獨立核數師。董事會及審核委員會隨後           objectivity of the Company’s independent auditor, and in
      已對該事項進行了檢討及審議，並同意更換獨立           order to prevent and mitigate familiarity threats that may
      核數師的建議。                         be raised over time due to increasingly close relationship
                                      of the independent auditor with the Company and the
                                      associated risks to performance standard arising from
                                      commercial dynamics, CRH took a more prudent approach
                                      and considered the change of independent auditor of
                                      the Company after three completed cycles of audits as a
                                      good corporate governance practice and measurement
                                      and so recommended a change of independent auditor
                                      to the Company. The Board and the Audit Committee
                                      have subsequently reviewed this matter and agreed the
                                      proposed change of independent auditor.

      後溢利至法定盈餘儲備，直至達到註冊資本的                  companies that is domiciled in the PRC is required to
      50%為止。轉撥至該儲備須於向股東作出股息                 allocate 10% of its profit after tax, to the statutory surplus
      分派前作出。                                reserve until the reserve reaches 50% of the registered
                                            capital. The transfer to this reserve must be made before
                                            the distribution of a dividend to shareholders. The statutory
                                            surplus reserve is non-distributable except that in the event

                                            certain restrictions set out in the relevant PRC regulations.
